<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the smallest number of coins I can be given in change when paying a bill for $\pounds 2.34$ with a $\pounds 5$ note.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the smallest number of coins I can be given in change when paying a bill for &#36;\pounds 2.34&#36; with a &#36;\pounds 5&#36; note.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- The coins available are: $1 \ \text{p}$, $2 \ \text{p}$, $5 \ \text{p}$, $10 \ \text{p}$, $20 \ \text{p}$, $50 \ \text{p}$, $\pounds 1$, $\pounds 2$.
</div>

                            &= \pounds 2.66 \\\\
\text {Coins received}      &= \pounds 2, 50 \ \text{p}, 10 \ \text{p}, 5 \ \text{p},1 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text {Number of coins}     &= 5
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Sophie and her friends decided to raise money for charity by baking and selling cookies.
They set themselves a target of $\pounds 200$ profit to raise.

Each cookie cost them $10 \ \text{p}$ to make and they sold them for $50 \ \text{p}$.

How many cookies did they need to sell to reach their target?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Sophie and her friends decided to raise money for charity by baking and selling cookies.
They set themselves a target of &#36;\pounds 200&#36; profit to raise.

Each cookie cost them &#36;10 \ \text{p}&#36; to make and they sold them for &#36;50 \ \text{p}&#36;.

How many cookies did they need to sell to reach their target?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

The cookie cost is given in pence and the target is given in pounds. Let's convert the target into pence.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total target}                     &= \pounds 200 \\\\
                                         &= 200 \times 100 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                         &= 20000 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text {Cost of each cookie}              &= 10 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text {Sale Price for each cookie}       &= 50 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text {Profit per cookie}                &= 50 - 10 \\\\
                                         &= 40 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of cookies needed}             &= \dfrac {\text {Total target}} {\text {Profit per cookie}} \\\\
                                             &= \dfrac {20000} {40} \\\\
                                             &= \dfrac {40 \times 500} {40} \\\\
                                             &= \dfrac {\cancel {40} \times 500} {\cancel {40}} \\\\
                                             &= 500
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: Alternate solution
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number of cookies $= c$

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
(50 - 10) \times c              &= 20000 \\\\
40c                             &= 20000 \\\\
c                               &= \dfrac {20000} {40} \\\\
c                               &= \dfrac {40 \times 500} {40} \\\\
c                               &= \dfrac {\cancel {40} \times 500} {\cancel {40}} \\\\
c                               &= 500
\end{aligned}
$

Number of cookies needed $= 500$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$500$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

                                         &= 20000 \ \text{p} \\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Katie's mum gives her $\pounds 10$.

She spends $\pounds 1.90$ on magazines, $\pounds 2.45$ on a birthday card and $75 \ \text{p}$ on chocolate. How much money does she have left? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Katie's mum gives her &#36;\pounds 10&#36;.

She spends &#36;\pounds 1.90&#36; on magazines, &#36;\pounds 2.45&#36; on a birthday card and &#36;75 \ \text{p}&#36; on chocolate. How much money does she have left? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Jake buys $2$ coffees, a milkshake and a cappuccino.

How much change does he receive from a $ \pounds 10?$

|      Drinks Menu     |                   |
|:--------------------:|:-----------------:|
|          Tea         |  $\pounds 1.40$   |
|         Coffee       |  $\pounds 1.60$   |       
|        Cappuccino    |  $\pounds 1.90$   |  
|     Hot Chocolate    |  $\pounds 1.50$   |
|     Fruit Juice      |  $\pounds 1.20$   |       
|        Milkshake     |  $\pounds 1.70$   |     
|        Smoothie      |  $\pounds 1.30$   |       
|        Fizzy Pop     |  $\pounds 1.00$   |      


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Jake buys &#36;2&#36; coffees, a milkshake and a cappuccino.

How much change does he receive from a &#36; \pounds 10?&#36;

|      Drinks Menu     |                   |
|:--------------------:|:-----------------:|
|          Tea         |  &#36;\pounds 1.40&#36;   |
|         Coffee       |  &#36;\pounds 1.60&#36;   |       
|        Cappuccino    |  &#36;\pounds 1.90&#36;   |  
|     Hot Chocolate    |  &#36;\pounds 1.50&#36;   |
|     Fruit Juice      |  &#36;\pounds 1.20&#36;   |       
|        Milkshake     |  &#36;\pounds 1.70&#36;   |     
|        Smoothie      |  &#36;\pounds 1.30&#36;   |       
|        Fizzy Pop     |  &#36;\pounds 1.00&#36;   |      

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 2 \times 1.60 + 1.70 + 1.90 \\\\
                        &= 3.20 + 3.60 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 6.80 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 10 - 6.80 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 3.20
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 3.20$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Decrease $\pounds 44$ by $10\%$.

- $\pounds 39.60$
- $\pounds 4.40$
- $\pounds 48.40$
- $\pounds 34$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Decrease &#36;\pounds 44&#36; by &#36;10\%&#36;.

- &#36;\pounds 39.60&#36;
- &#36;\pounds 4.40&#36;
- &#36;\pounds 48.40&#36;
- &#36;\pounds 34&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the amount is reduced by $10 \%$, then the reduced value will be $100 - 10 = 90 \%$ of the original value. Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 90 \% \ \text {of} \ \text 44 \\\\
&= \dfrac {90} {100} \times 44 \\\\
&= \dfrac {90 \times 44} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3960} {100} \\\\
&= \pounds 39.60 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: Alternate solution
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 44 - 10 \% \ \text {of} \ 44 \\\\
&= 44 - \dfrac {10} {100} \times 44 \\\\
&= 44 - \dfrac {10 \times 44} {100} \\\\
&= 44 - \dfrac {440} {100} \\\\
&= 44 - 4.40 \\\\
&= \pounds 39.60 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>A</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 39.60$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If the amount is reduced by &#36;10 \%&#36;, then the reduced value will be &#36;100 - 10 = 90 \%&#36; of the original value. Using that, we get

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
&= 90 \% \ \text {of} \ \text 44 \\\\
&= \dfrac {90} {100} \times 44 \\\\
&= \dfrac {90 \times 44} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3960} {100} \\\\
&= \pounds 39.60 
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find $40\%$ of $\pounds 620$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find &#36;40\%&#36; of &#36;\pounds 620&#36;.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 40\% \text{ of } \pounds 620 \\\\
&= \dfrac{40}{100} \times 620 \\\\
&= \dfrac{40 \times 620}{100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{4\cancel{0} \times 62\cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}}  \\\\
&= 4 \times 62  \\\\
&= \pounds 248
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find $20 \%$ of $\pounds 80$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find &#36;20 \%&#36; of &#36;\pounds 80&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 20\% \text{ of } \pounds 80 \\\\
&= \dfrac{20}{100} \times 80 \\\\
&= \dfrac{20 \times 80}{100}  \\\\
&= \dfrac{2\cancel{0} \times 8\cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0}\cancel{0}}  \\\\
&= 2\times 8\\\\
&= \pounds 16
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find $\dfrac{2}{5}$ of $\pounds 40$. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find &#36;\dfrac{2}{5}&#36; of &#36;\pounds 40&#36;. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {2}{5} \text{ of } \pounds 40 \\\\
&= \dfrac{2 \times 40}{5} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2 \times 5 \times 8}{5} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2 \times \cancel{5} \times 8}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
&= 2 \times 8 \\\\
&= \pounds 16
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 16$

</div>
</div>

&= \dfrac {2}{5} \text{ of } \pounds 40 \\\\
&= \dfrac{2 \times 40}{5} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2 \times 5 \times 8}{5} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2 \times \cancel{5} \times 8}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
&= 2 \times 8 \\\\
&= \pounds 16
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Ruby has collected thirty-five $5 \ \text{p}$ coins for charity.

How much more does she need to reach $\pounds 5?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Ruby has collected thirty-five &#36;5 \ \text{p}&#36; coins for charity.

How much more does she need to reach &#36;\pounds 5?&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Amount collected}    &= 35 \times 5 \\\\
                            &= 175 \ \text{p}  \\\\
                            &= \pounds 1.75 \\\\
\text {Amount needed}       &= 5 - 1.75 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 3.25
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I buy $2$ cakes for $45 \ \text{p}$ each and a drink for $90 \ \text{p}$. 
What change will I have from a $\pounds 5$ note?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I buy &#36;2&#36; cakes for &#36;45 \ \text{p}&#36; each and a drink for &#36;90 \ \text{p}&#36;. 
What change will I have from a &#36;\pounds 5&#36; note?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 2 \times 45 + 90 \\\\
                        &= 90 + 90 \\\\
                        &= 180 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 1.80 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 5 - 1.80 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 3.20
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 3.20$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I buy $6$ packets of crisps from the $\pounds 1.99$ store where everything costs $\pounds 1.99$. How much change do I get 
from $\pounds 20?$

-  $6 \ \text{p}$ 
-  $\pounds 6.99$ 
-  $\pounds 11.94$ 
-  $\pounds 4.06$ 
-  $\pounds 8.06$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I buy &#36;6&#36; packets of crisps from the &#36;\pounds 1.99&#36; store where everything costs &#36;\pounds 1.99&#36;. How much change do I get 
from &#36;\pounds 20?&#36;

-  &#36;6 \ \text{p}&#36; 
-  &#36;\pounds 6.99&#36; 
-  &#36;\pounds 11.94&#36; 
-  &#36;\pounds 4.06&#36; 
-  &#36;\pounds 8.06&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 6 \times 1.99 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 11.94 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 20 - 11.94 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 8.06
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$\pounds 8.06$

</div>

inherits parent tags: yes
</span>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I set off with a $\pounds 10$ note to buy a book costing $\pounds 4.95$, a cake 
costing  $\pounds 1.30$ and a new pen. I got  $\pounds 2.25$ change. 

How much did the pen cost?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I set off with a &#36;\pounds 10&#36; note to buy a book costing &#36;\pounds 4.95&#36;, a cake 
costing  &#36;\pounds 1.30&#36; and a new pen. I got  &#36;\pounds 2.25&#36; change. 

How much did the pen cost?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of pen}     &= 10 - 4.95 - 1.30 - 2.25 \\\\
                        &= 5.05 - 1.30 - 2.25 \\\\
                        &= 3.75 - 2.25 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 1.50
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 1.50$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Six children each had an equal share of a sum of money.

They each received  $\pounds 3.18$ and there was $2 \ \text{p}$ left over.

How much was the sum of money?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Six children each had an equal share of a sum of money.

They each received  &#36;\pounds 3.18&#36; and there was &#36;2 \ \text{p}&#36; left over.

How much was the sum of money?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total money}     &= 6 \times \pounds 3.18 + 2 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 19.08 + \pounds 0.02 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 19.10
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 19.10$

</div>

inherits parent tags: yes
</span>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Autnie Jean is saving all her $5 \ \text{p}$ coins in a jar.

The total amount of money in the jar is  $\pounds 18.55$.

How many coins are in the jar?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Autnie Jean is saving all her &#36;5 \ \text{p}&#36; coins in a jar.

The total amount of money in the jar is  &#36;\pounds 18.55&#36;.

How many coins are in the jar?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total coins}     &= \dfrac {\pounds 18.55} {5 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1855 \ \text{p}} {5 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {5 \times 371} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 371} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
                        &= 371
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$371$

</div>
</div>

                        &= \dfrac {5 \times 371} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 371} {\cancel {5}} \\\\

</code></pre>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A group of $30$ adults and $16$ children paid  $\pounds 408$ in total to 
watch a football match. Each child ticket cost  $\pounds 3$.

What was the cost of each adult ticket?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A group of &#36;30&#36; adults and &#36;16&#36; children paid  &#36;\pounds 408&#36; in total to 
watch a football match. Each child ticket cost  &#36;\pounds 3&#36;.

What was the cost of each adult ticket?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the cost of an adult ticket (in $\pounds$) $= a$

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
30 \times a + 16 \times 3   &= 408 \\\\
30a + 48                    &= 408 \\\\
30a                         &= 408 - 48 \\\\
30a                         &= 360 \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {360} {30} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {12 \times 30} {30} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel {30}} {\cancel {30}} \\\\
a                           &= \pounds 12
\end{aligned}
$

Cost of an adult ticket $= \pounds 12$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 12$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a sale, prices are reduced by $15 \%$.

What is the sale price of a hoody that originally cost  $\pounds 30?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In a sale, prices are reduced by &#36;15 \%&#36;.

What is the sale price of a hoody that originally cost  &#36;\pounds 30?&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $15 \%$, then the sale price will be $100 - 15 = 85 \%$ of the original price.

Using that, we get

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Sale price}      &= 85 \% \ \text {of} \ 30 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{85}{100} \times 30 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{85 \times 30}{100} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{2550}{100} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 25.50
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 25.50$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Guy has twice as many stickers as Asif and five more than Kayal.

They have $35$ stickers altogether.

How many stickers does Guy have?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Guy has twice as many stickers as Asif and five more than Kayal.

They have &#36;35&#36; stickers altogether.

How many stickers does Guy have?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number of stickers Asif has $= a$

Number of stickers Guy has $= 2a$

Number of stickers Kyal has $= 2a - 5$

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
a + 2a + 2a - 5             &= 35 \\\\
5a                          &= 35 + 5 \\\\
5a                          &= 40 \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {40}{5} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {5 \times 8}{5} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {\cancel{5} \times 8}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
a                           &= 8 \\\\
\text{Stickers Guy has}     &= 2a \\\\
                            &= 2 \times 8 \\\\
                            &= 16
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$16$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I set off with a $\pounds 10$ note to buy a book 
costing $\pounds 4.95$, a cake costing $\pounds 1.30$ and a new pen. 
I got $\pounds 2.25$ change. 

How much did the pen cost?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I set off with a &#36;\pounds 10&#36; note to buy a book 
costing &#36;\pounds 4.95&#36;, a cake costing &#36;\pounds 1.30&#36; and a new pen. 
I got &#36;\pounds 2.25&#36; change. 

How much did the pen cost?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of pen}     &= 10 - 4.95 - 1.30 - 2.25 \\\\
                        &= 5.05 - 1.30 - 2.25 \\\\
                        &= 3.75 - 2.25 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 1.50
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 1.50$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Six children each had an equal share of a sum of money. 

They each received $\pounds 3.18$ and there was $2 \ \text{p}$ left over. 

How much was the sum of money?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Six children each had an equal share of a sum of money. 

They each received &#36;\pounds 3.18&#36; and there was &#36;2 \ \text{p}&#36; left over. 

How much was the sum of money?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total money}     &= 6 \times \pounds 3.18 + 2 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 19.08 + \pounds 0.02 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 19.10
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$\pounds 19.10$

</span>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Auntie Jean is saving all her $5 \ \text{p}$ coins in a jar. 

The total amount of money in the jar is $\pounds 18.55$. 

How many coins are in the jar?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Auntie Jean is saving all her &#36;5 \ \text{p}&#36; coins in a jar. 

The total amount of money in the jar is &#36;\pounds 18.55&#36;. 

How many coins are in the jar?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total coins}     &= \dfrac {\pounds 18.55} {5 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1855 \ \text{p}} {5 \ \text{p}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {5 \times 371} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 371} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
                        &= 371 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$371$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="uuid">

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #15803d; border-radius: 999px; background: #dcfce7; color: #14532d; padding: 0.24rem 0.7rem; font-size: 0.95rem; font-weight: 700;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A group of $30$ adults and $16$ children paid $\pounds 408$ in total to watch a football match. 
Each child ticket cost $\pounds 3$.

What was the cost of each adult ticket?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A group of &#36;30&#36; adults and &#36;16&#36; children paid &#36;\pounds 408&#36; in total to watch a football match. 
Each child ticket cost &#36;\pounds 3&#36;.

What was the cost of each adult ticket?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the cost of an adult ticket $= a$

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
30 a + 16 \times 3          &= 408 \\\\
30a + 48                    &= 408 \\\\
30a                         &= 408 - 48 \\\\
30a                         &= 360 \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {360} {30} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {12 \times 30} {30} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel {30}} {\cancel {30}} \\\\
a                           &= \pounds 12
\end{aligned}
$

Cost of an adult ticket $= \pounds 12$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 12$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a sale, prices are reduced by $15 \%$.

What is the sale price of a hoody that originally cost $\pounds 30?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In a sale, prices are reduced by &#36;15 \%&#36;.

What is the sale price of a hoody that originally cost &#36;\pounds 30?&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

If the price is reduced by $15 \%$, then the sale price will be $100 - 15 = 85 \%$ of the original price.

Using this we get,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Sale price}   &= 85 \% \ \text {of} \ 30 \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {85} {100} \times 30 \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {85 \times 30} {100} \\\\
                     &= \dfrac {2550} {100} \\\\
                     &= \pounds 25.50
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 25.50$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If the price is reduced by &#36;15 \%&#36;, then the sale price will be &#36;100 - 15 = 85 \%&#36; of the original price.

Using this we get,

</code></pre>

</div>

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Guy has twice as many stickers as Asif and five more than Kayal. 

They have $35$ stickers altogether.

How many stickers does Guy have?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Guy has twice as many stickers as Asif and five more than Kayal. 

They have &#36;35&#36; stickers altogether.

How many stickers does Guy have?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number of stickers Asif has $= a$

Number of stickers Guy has $= 2a$

Number of stickers Kyal has $= 2a - 5$

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
a + 2a + 2a - 5             &= 35 \\\\
5a                          &= 35 + 5 \\\\
5a                          &= 40 \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {40}{5} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {5 \times 8}{5} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {\cancel{5} \times 8}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
a                           &= 8 \\\\
\text{Stickers Guy has}     &= 2a \\\\
                            &= 2 \times 8 \\\\
                            &= 16
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$16$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</div>

</div>

Number of stickers Guy has &#36;= 2a&#36;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
a + 2a + 2a - 5             &= 35 \\\\
5a                          &= 35 + 5 \\\\
5a                          &= 40 \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {40}{5} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {5 \times 8}{5} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {\cancel{5} \times 8}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
a                           &= 8 \\\\
\text{Stickers Guy has}     &= 2a \\\\
                            &= 2 \times 8 \\\\
                            &= 16
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Neruka bought a pen costing $\pounds 4.93$, a pencil costing $24 \ \text{p}$ and a ruler costing $58 \ \text{p}$. How much did she spend altogether?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Neruka bought a pen costing &#36;\pounds 4.93&#36;, a pencil costing &#36;24 \ \text{p}&#36; and a ruler costing &#36;58 \ \text{p}&#36;. How much did she spend altogether?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Money spent}  &= \pounds 4.93 + 24 \ \text{p} + 58 \ \text{p} \\\\
                    &= \pounds 4.93 + \pounds 0.24 + \pounds 0.58 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 5.75
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 5.75$

<div class="uuid">
<p>9242988C-D102-4109-9B9F-6055B5BB98FB</p>
</div>
<div class="papername">
<p>city-of-london-freemens-school--11-plus--maths--9999--sample-paper-1:3:2</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>Waffles Woof</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_ng1</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_prns</p>
</div>
<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">
<ul style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: flex-start; gap: 0.55rem; margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none;">
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
tmoney
</span>
</li>
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
tcoin
</span>
</li>
</ul>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A can of lemonade from a vending machine costs $65 \ \text{p}$. 
The table below shows the coins that were collected from the machine in one day.

|      Coins      | Number of Coins |
|:---------------:|:---------------:|
| $50 \ \text{p}$   |      $22$       |
| $20 \ \text{p}$   |      $18$       |
| $10 \ \text{p}$   |      $33$       |
|  $5 \ \text{p}$   |      $41$       |

How many cans of lemonade were sold that day?

-   $\pounds 20.85$ 
-   $\pounds 19.95$
-   $31$ 
-   $30$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A can of lemonade from a vending machine costs &#36;65 \ \text{p}&#36;. 
The table below shows the coins that were collected from the machine in one day.

|      Coins      | Number of Coins |
|:---------------:|:---------------:|
| &#36;50 \ \text{p}&#36;   |      &#36;22&#36;       |
| &#36;20 \ \text{p}&#36;   |      &#36;18&#36;       |
| &#36;10 \ \text{p}&#36;   |      &#36;33&#36;       |
|  &#36;5 \ \text{p}&#36;   |      &#36;41&#36;       |

How many cans of lemonade were sold that day?

-   &#36;\pounds 20.85&#36; 
-   &#36;\pounds 19.95&#36;
-   &#36;31&#36; 
-   &#36;30&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total money}             &= 22 \times 50 + 18 \times 20 + 33 \times 10 + 41 \times 5 \\\\
                                &= 1100 + 360 + 330 + 205 \\\\
                                &= 1995 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text {Number of cans sold}     &= \dfrac {1995} {65} \\\\
                                &= 30 \ \text {remainder} \ 5 \\\\
                                &\approx 30
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>D</p>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A can of lemonade from a vending machine costs $65 \ \text{p}$. The table below shows the coins that were collected from the machine in one day.

|      Coins      | Number of Coins |
|:---------------:|:---------------:|
|  $50 \ \text{p}$  |      $22$       |
|  $20 \ \text{p}$  |      $18$       |
|  $10 \ \text{p}$  |      $33$       |
|  $5 \ \text{p}$   |      $41$       |

How many cans of lemonade were sold that day?

- $\pounds 20.85 $
- $\pounds 19.95 $ 
- $31$ 
- $30$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A can of lemonade from a vending machine costs &#36;65 \ \text{p}&#36;. The table below shows the coins that were collected from the machine in one day.

|      Coins      | Number of Coins |
|:---------------:|:---------------:|
|  &#36;50 \ \text{p}&#36;  |      &#36;22&#36;       |
|  &#36;20 \ \text{p}&#36;  |      &#36;18&#36;       |
|  &#36;10 \ \text{p}&#36;  |      &#36;33&#36;       |
|  &#36;5 \ \text{p}&#36;   |      &#36;41&#36;       |

How many cans of lemonade were sold that day?

- &#36;\pounds 20.85 &#36;
- &#36;\pounds 19.95 &#36; 
- &#36;31&#36; 
- &#36;30&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total money}         &= 22 \times 50 + 18 \times 20 + 33 \times 10 + 41 \times 5 \\\\
                            &= 1100 + 360 + 330 + 205 \\\\
                            &= 1995 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text {Number of cans sold} &= \dfrac {1995} {65} \\\\
                            &= 30 \ \text {remainder} \ 5 \\\\
                            &\approx 30
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<p>D</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$30$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I buy $7$ drinks at $72\ \text{p}$ each, and $9$ sandwiches at $\pounds 1.21$ each. 

How much change do I get from a $\pounds 20$ note? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I buy &#36;7&#36; drinks at &#36;72\ \text{p}&#36; each, and &#36;9&#36; sandwiches at &#36;\pounds 1.21&#36; each. 

How much change do I get from a &#36;\pounds 20&#36; note? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}         &= 7 \times 72 \ \text{p} +  9 \times \pounds 1.21 \\\\
                            &= 7 \times \pounds 0.72 + 9 \times \pounds 1.21 \\\\
                            &= 5.04 + 10.89 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 15.93 \\\\
\text {Change received}     &= 20 - 15.93 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 4.07
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 4.07$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}         &= 7 \times 72 \ \text{p} +  9 \times \pounds 1.21 \\\\
                            &= 7 \times \pounds 0.72 + 9 \times \pounds 1.21 \\\\
                            &= 5.04 + 10.89 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 15.93 \\\\
\text {Change received}     &= 20 - 15.93 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 4.07
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Billy went shopping and bought $3$ computer games that cost $\pounds 25.50$ each and $2$ doughnuts that cost $\pounds 1.30$ each.

How much change did Billy get from $\pounds 100?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Billy went shopping and bought &#36;3&#36; computer games that cost &#36;\pounds 25.50&#36; each and &#36;2&#36; doughnuts that cost &#36;\pounds 1.30&#36; each.

How much change did Billy get from &#36;\pounds 100?&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}         &= 3 \times 25.50 + 2 \times 1.30 \\\\
                            &= 76.50 + 2.60 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 79.10 \\\\
\text {Change received}     &= 100 - 79.10 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 20.90
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 20.90$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                            &= 76.50 + 2.60 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 79.10 \\\\
\text {Change received}     &= 100 - 79.10 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 20.90
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Refer to the following school canteen price list for this question:

|Pasta Salad  |$95 \ \text{p}$|
|:---:|:----:|
|Baguette  |$85 \ \text{p}$|
|Apple  |$40 \ \text{p}$|
|Fruit Crumble  |$\pounds 1.15$|
|Curry and Rice  |$\pounds 2.30$|

Mr. Sanders wants to buy a class set of $25$ packed lunches - each with a pasta salad, a baguette and an apple.

How much will he have to pay in total?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Refer to the following school canteen price list for this question:

|Pasta Salad  |&#36;95 \ \text{p}&#36;|
|:---:|:----:|
|Baguette  |&#36;85 \ \text{p}&#36;|
|Apple  |&#36;40 \ \text{p}&#36;|
|Fruit Crumble  |&#36;\pounds 1.15&#36;|
|Curry and Rice  |&#36;\pounds 2.30&#36;|

Mr. Sanders wants to buy a class set of &#36;25&#36; packed lunches - each with a pasta salad, a baguette and an apple.

How much will he have to pay in total?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= 25 \times (95 + 85 + 40) \\\\
                    &= 25 \times 220  \\\\
                    &= 5500 \ \text{p}  \\\\
                    &= \pounds 55
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 55$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

                    &= 25 \times 220  \\\\
                    &= 5500 \ \text{p}  \\\\
                    &= \pounds 55
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How much money will TJ recieve if he sells all $15$ bars at $35 \ \text{p}?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How much money will TJ recieve if he sells all &#36;15&#36; bars at &#36;35 \ \text{p}?&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money received}  &= 15 \times 35 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= 15 \times \pounds 0.35 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.25
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 5.25$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How much did TJ pay for each bar when he bought the box?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How much did TJ pay for each bar when he bought the box?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Cost of a bar}   &= \dfrac {\pounds 4.05} {15} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {4.05 \times 100 \ \text{p}} {15} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {405} {15} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {15 \times 27} {15} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {15} \times 27} {\cancel {15}} \\\\
                        &= 27 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$27 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

TJ also has two types of cereal snacks: raisin bars and nut bars. Each raisin bar is $12 \ \text{p}$ 
more to buy than a nut bar. Nine raisin bars and twelve nut bars cost $\pounds 5.28$.

How much more do nine raisin bars cost than nine nut bars?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">TJ also has two types of cereal snacks: raisin bars and nut bars. Each raisin bar is &#36;12 \ \text{p}&#36; 
more to buy than a nut bar. Nine raisin bars and twelve nut bars cost &#36;\pounds 5.28&#36;.

How much more do nine raisin bars cost than nine nut bars?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

We know that each raisin bar costs $12 \ \text{p}$ more than a nut bar.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}                          &= 9 \times 12 \\\\
                                            &= 108 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                            &= \pounds 1.08
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 1.08$

</div>
</div>

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How much does a raisin bar cost?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the cost of a nut bar (in pence) $= a$

Cost of a raisin bar (in pence) $= a + 12$

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
9 \times (a + 12) + 12 \times a     &= \pounds 5.28 \\\\
9a + 108 + 12a                      &= 528 \ \text{p} \\\\
21a + 108                           &= 528 \\\\
21a                                 &= 528 - 108 \\\\
21a                                 &= 420 \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {420} {21} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {20 \times 21} {21} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {20 \times \cancel {21}} {\cancel {21}} \\\\
a                                   &= 20 \ \text{p} \\\\
\text {Cost of raisin bar}          &= a + 12 \\\\
                                    &= 20 + 12 \\\\
                                    &= 32 \ \text{p}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$32 \ \text{p}$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

&= \dfrac {620} {100} \\\\
&= \pounds 6.20
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate $11\%$ of $\pounds 62$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 11 \% \ \text {of} \ \pounds 62 \\\\
&= \dfrac {11} {100} \times 62 \\\\
&= \dfrac {11 \times 62} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {682} {100} \\\\
&= \pounds 6.82
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A coat costing $\pounds 62$ is reduced in a sale by $\pounds 12.40$. 

What percentage discount is this?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A coat costing &#36;\pounds 62&#36; is reduced in a sale by &#36;\pounds 12.40&#36;. 

What percentage discount is this?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
\text {\% Discount}     &= \dfrac {\text {Discount}} {\text {Original price}} \times 100 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {12.40} {62} \times 100 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {12.40 \times 100} {62} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {1240} {62} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {62 \times 20} {62} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{62} \times 20} {\cancel{62}} \\\\
                                &= 20 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20 \%$

</div>
</div>

                                &= \dfrac {1240} {62} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {62 \times 20} {62} \\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Ola is planning to complete a sponsored run for Red Nose Day.

He collected a number of different promises, from his family, on his sponsor form.

|    Mum    |                                             $50 \ \text{p}$ per completed mile                                               |
|:---------:|:--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------:|
|    Dad    |                                     $ \pounds 5$ plus $25 \ \text{p}$ per completed mile                                     |
|    Nan    |                                                       $ \pounds 10$                                                        |
|  Grandad  | $10 \ \text{p}$ for the first mile, $20 \ \text{p}$ for the second mile, $30 \ \text{p}$ for the third mile, and so on $ \ldots $|
| Uncle Jay |                                                       $\pounds 15$                                                         |

He hopes to complete either $8$ or $10 \ \text{miles}$.

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How much extra will Ola collect, in total, if he completes the extra $2 \ \text{miles}$ and runs $10 \ \text{miles}$ (rather than $8 \ \text{miles}$)?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How much extra will Ola collect, in total, if he completes the extra &#36;2 \ \text{miles}&#36; and runs &#36;10 \ \text{miles}&#36; (rather than &#36;8 \ \text{miles}&#36;)?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>

<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
