%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money from mum}      &= 10 \times 50  \\\\
                            &= 500 \ \text{p}  \\\\
                            &= \pounds 5 \\\\
\text {Money from dad}      &= \pounds 5 + 10 \times 25 \ \text{p} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 5 + 10 \times \pounds 0.25 \\\\
                            &= 5 + 2.50 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 7.50 \\\\
\text {Money from grandad}  &= 360  + 90  + 100  \\\\
                            &= 550 \ \text{p} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 5.50 \\\\
\text {Money collected}     &= \text{Mum} + \text{Dad} + \text{Nan} + \text{Grandad} + \text{Uncle} \\\\
                            &= 5 + 7.50 + 10 + 5.50 + 15 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 43 \\\\
\text {Extra money}         &= 43 - 39.60 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 3.40
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$\pounds 3.40$

**Question**

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Robbie saves $\pounds 3.50$ every week. How much does he save in a year?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Robbie saves &#36;\pounds 3.50&#36; every week. How much does he save in a year?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Savings in a year}      &= 52 \times 3.50 \\\\
                               &= \pounds 182
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 182$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How much change would there be from a $\pounds 20$ note for Alice, Owen, Mum, Dad and Gran if they use the car park?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How much change would there be from a &#36;\pounds 20&#36; note for Alice, Owen, Mum, Dad and Gran if they use the car park?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Money spent}      &= 2 \times 1.60 + 3 \times 2.40 + 1.50 \\\\
                        &= 3.20 + 7.20 + 1.50 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 11.90 \\\\
\text{Change received}  &= 20 - 11.90 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 8.10
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 8.10$

</div>
</div>

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
tmoney
</span>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I buy $11$ chocolate bars costing $38 \ \text{p}$ each. How much change would I get from $\pounds 10?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I buy &#36;11&#36; chocolate bars costing &#36;38 \ \text{p}&#36; each. How much change would I get from &#36;\pounds 10?&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text {Money spent}         &= 11 \times 38 \\\\
                            &= 418 \ \text{p} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 4.18 \\\\
\text {Change recieved}     &= 10 - 4.18 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 5.82
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 5.82$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_note_md_unit_no_dot
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;

                            &= 418 \ \text{p} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 4.18 \\\\

                            &= \pounds 5.82
\end{aligned}

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I buy $11$ chocolate bars costing $27 \ \text{p}$ each. How much change would I get from $\pounds 5?$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I buy &#36;11&#36; chocolate bars costing &#36;27 \ \text{p}&#36; each. How much change would I get from &#36;\pounds 5?&#36; 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text {Money spent}         &= 11 \times 27 \\\\
                            &= 297 \ \text{p} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 2.97 \\\\
\text {Change recieved}     &= 5 - 2.97 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 2.03
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 2.03$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is $\dfrac{3}{4}$ of $\ \pounds 4.48?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is &#36;\dfrac{3}{4}&#36; of &#36;\ \pounds 4.48?&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \ \text {of} \ \pounds 4.48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 4.48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 4.48} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13.44} {4} \\\\
&= \pounds 3.36
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 3.36$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I buy $13$ chocolate bars costing $23 \ \text{p}$ each. How much change would I get from $\pounds 5?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I buy &#36;13&#36; chocolate bars costing &#36;23 \ \text{p}&#36; each. How much change would I get from &#36;\pounds 5?&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}      &= 13 \times 23 \\\\
                        &= 299 \ \text{p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 2.99 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 5 - 2.99 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 2.01
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 2.01$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What percentage of $\pounds 5$ is $\pounds 1?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What percentage of &#36;\pounds 5&#36; is &#36;\pounds 1?&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Percentage}               &= \dfrac {1} {5} \times 100 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {100} {5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {5 \times 20} {5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{5} \times 20} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{5} \times 20} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
                                &= 20 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$20 \%$

</div>
</div>

</div>

                                &= \dfrac {100} {5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {5 \times 20} {5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{5} \times 20} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{5} \times 20} {\cancel{5}} \\\\
                                &= 20 \%
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How much change would you get from $\pounds 10$ if you bought $7$ $\text{Chocco}^{\text{TM}}$ bars at $78 \ \text{p}$ each?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How much change would you get from &#36;\pounds 10&#36; if you bought &#36;7&#36; &#36;\text{Chocco}^{\text{TM}}&#36; bars at &#36;78 \ \text{p}&#36; each?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.
_no_dot
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text {Money spent}             &= 7 \times 78 \\\\
                                &= 546 \ \text{p} \\\\
                                &= \pounds 5.46 \\\\
\text {Change recieved}         &= 10 - 5.46 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 4.54
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 4.54$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is $15 \%$ of $\pounds 120?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is &#36;15 \%&#36; of &#36;\pounds 120?&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 15 \% \ \text {of} \ \pounds 120 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15} {100} \times 120 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 120} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 5 \times 6 \times 20} {5 \times 20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {5} \times 6 \times \cancel {20}} {\cancel {5} \times \cancel {20}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 6 \\\\
&= \pounds 18
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 18$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

&= 15 \% \ \text {of} \ \pounds 120 \\\\

&= \dfrac {15 \times 120} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 5 \times 6 \times 20} {5 \times 20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {5} \times 6 \times \cancel {20}} {\cancel {5} \times \cancel {20}} \\\\

\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
